Research & Engineering Lineage · Evolved-AMA

Autonomous Agents

Autonomous Agents — a source-grounded lineage account with every original property individually addressable.

AMA family and fourth-stage synthesis · Four-unit source package · Eight-component assessment

Two scientific layers

This reader covers the 70 historical public Autonomous Agents rows, frozen as EAA-2026-09-06-r1. The later accepted AMA-L1 subject also selects 70 identities, but it is a separately revised, reviewed scientific object. Its subject SHA-256 is 594dfaa5c71a8e907b437a22d2e3bfb717dc078565048c040538df6cda3a7bb6; both independent accepted reviews and qualification are pinned in SOURCE_MAP.json. Equal population sizes do not establish equal scientific text. The complete current account, adjudications, actual review observations and limits are retained privately. Publishing their detailed interpretations requires AMA-DISCLOSE-AMA-L1, which remains PENDING.

The public predecessor's actual scientific contribution

Autonomous Agents studies an entity's continuing relation to its environment: what it can observe, decide and do; which goals remain effective; which actions lie inside its delegation; and how effects, failures and revisions are examined. The historical study's “Evolved” label describes its analytical reconstruction. It does not identify a single historical school or an established consensus. The lineage grew through several interacting traditions: situated control, symbolic planning, practical reasoning and BDI, decision making under uncertainty, learning, mixed initiative and later language-mediated tool use. These branches answer different problems and remain alternatives. Historical public intake · Coverage and genealogy

The sharp first predicate is action attribution at an explicit boundary, EAA-001. An evaluator should be able to distinguish an agent action from an environmental event or another actor's action. This differs from EAA-004's delegated scope, from EAA-021/022's grounded and permitted action, and from EAA-023's evidence of the resulting effect. A calendar operation can be technically callable, within the assistant's intended plan, and still lack the particular user's permission. A queue acknowledgement can establish receipt while the effect remains unknown. Conversely, an adequate atomic return contract can supply the relevant effect evidence without an extra confirmation procedure. These examples illustrate the historical predicates; they are not new deployed measurements. Historical ledger: EAA-001, 004, 021–023

Observation and representation also have separate truth conditions. EAA-005–008 distinguish observations, world state, belief and evidential provenance. An internally consistent belief can still rest on an invalid sensor proxy. A model-relative sufficient statistic does not establish the model's correctness. Situated reaction can avoid unnecessary representational cost; hidden state can make a memoryless policy inadequate. The historical ledger retains both rejected universal extremes, including mandatory complete central representation and universal sufficiency of pure reaction, rather than turning one successful architecture into a compulsory design. EAA-005–008, 016, 051, 058–059

Goals and intentions require a horizon. Persistence can prevent repeated deliberation, while reconsideration becomes necessary when a goal loses relevance, feasibility or legitimacy. A maintenance condition does not become permanently true after one favorable observation. Planning, checking and recovery share the task's finite time and resource envelope. EAA-026–028 therefore concern conditional allocation of deliberation, not installation of an optimizing metareasoner for every task. EAA-010–015, 026–028

Memory and learning are conditional mechanisms. EAA-030 concerns a retained experience's origin, evidential status and legitimate later use; EAA-031 concerns selection and retirement for an actual purpose. Repeating a generated success story does not verify it. A frequent-success rule can discard the only warning of a rare severe failure. Current state updates, learned parameters, textual lessons and changes to the criterion itself remain distinct. The evidence does not justify universal memory, reflection or self-revision. EAA-062 retains general open-ended self-revision assurance as unresolved. EAA-029–035, 054, 060–062, 070

Human control is similarly plural: an operative stop path, an interruption-neutral learning result and a willingness-to-defer result have different bearers and assumptions. A person who receives a request too late cannot intervene before an irreversible effect. A nominal supervisor can lack information, attention, skill or usable authority. The useful contribution is a feasible decision and effect boundary, with nonresponse and recovery considered. EAA-037–042

Composition, criticism and adequate alternatives

The historical account offers six alternatives: a minimal situated controller; a stateful deliberative or BDI agent; a hybrid controller; a mixed-initiative assistant; a bounded language-model tool user; and a protected learning branch. Each selection must earn its mechanisms against the actual task and cheaper adequate alternatives. A direct service, fixed workflow or small controller remains admissible. The configuration family was not evaluated as one universally superior system. Historical composition

Three inherited derived criteria remain individually named: EAA-067's coherent action basis; EAA-068's failure-attributed revision; and EAA-069's task/scope-preserving repair. Their useful relation is that a repair should address the supported failure while preserving the still-effective goal and delegation. A replacement means can be authorized without a fresh approval; changing the beneficiary or permitted scope is a different decision. The row population also preserves duplicates, ceremonial propositions, contested branches and rejected universals. Those are scientific findings, not missing features. Historical ledger

The public historical source distinguishes a successful mission or benchmark from field prevalence, task reliability from believability, and repeat-attempt success from dependable one-shot behavior. It also keeps source access and edition limits. This reader carries those limits and supplies full property navigation; it does not requalify the literature or claim that every primary source was freshly read. Historical public intake

Separate native responsibility

Published, canonically unallocated R0045 / #233 contains the earlier bounded documentary attribution qualification. Its case separates assistant requests, a human owner's decision and external execution observations; runtime remained unchanged. That native record is a separate target/consumer layer, not acceptance of all 70 scientific mechanisms. The exact per-property historical target routes are in OWNER_MAP.json. Existing R0045 qualification

The rough AMA-L1 responsibility is to examine a future exact action-attribution, delegation, observation or change-assurance claim only when a real current consumer and material residual justify it. R0033/#207 retains governor/child authority; R0035/#209 retains dependency joins and single-writer currentness; R0036/#210 retains scoped lanes and parent custody; R0037/#211 retains nested control; R002D/#197 retains interface/realization evidence; R001F/#158 retains any later executor-ready work-order representation. No issue is reopened or allocated by this documentation. Four rough responsibilities

EAA-001 — Explicit agent–environment boundary (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S001; DATE: 1995-06; WORK: Intelligent Agents: Theory and Practice; VERSION: Knowledge Engineering Review 10(2), 115–152; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S002; DATE: 1997; WORK: Is it an Agent, or just a Program?: A Taxonomy for Autonomous Agents; VERSION: ATAL 1996 workshop; LNCS 1193 proceedings pp.21–35; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S003; DATE: 1995; WORK: A Formal Framework for Agency and Autonomy; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.254–260; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Competing weak/strong, situated-persistent and motivation-based definitions, not one necessary-and-sufficient academic consensus.
Development
Retain an explicit operational boundary while preserving alternative definitions rather than declaring one universal essence.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain an explicit operational boundary while preserving alternative definitions rather than declaring one universal essence.; OPERATION: Declare the entity, environmental variables it can observe or affect, objective attribution, persistence and the operative definition of agency. Keep interface boundaries distinct from metaphysical claims.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: For a pure function, describe input/output computation without forcing an agent label. A simple controller may satisfy a minimal situated definition.
Problem
Calling any automated output agency conceals whether there is an environment, an objective and any effective action.
Mechanism
Declare the entity, environmental variables it can observe or affect, objective attribution, persistence and the operative definition of agency. Keep interface boundaries distinct from metaphysical claims.
Trigger and context
Comparing systems, assigning responsibility or claiming autonomous capability.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Architect, evaluator and person interpreting capability claims. An identifiable task and observation/action interface; no requirement for a human-like self-model.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
An evaluator can tell which changes are agent actions, environmental events, or actions of another actor.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
For a pure function, describe input/output computation without forcing an agent label. A simple controller may satisfy a minimal situated definition.
Historical disposition
STRONGLY_RETAINED
Failure modes
The definitions disagree: internally motivated autonomy is not identical to externally goal-directed situated persistence. A tidy boundary can still omit environmental dependencies.; Introduced cost or harm: Modelling effort and risk of freezing an arbitrary boundary.

Assumption sensitivity
Boundary adequacy is relative to the causal question; nested agents and environments can require different resolutions.
Unresolved questions
Which definition is being used, and does the demonstrated system actually meet it?; Boundary adequacy is relative to the causal question; nested agents and environments can require different resolutions.

EAA-002 — Task-relative autonomy dimensions (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S001; DATE: 1995-06; WORK: Intelligent Agents: Theory and Practice; VERSION: Knowledge Engineering Review 10(2), 115–152; author HTML; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S003; DATE: 1995; WORK: A Formal Framework for Agency and Autonomy; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.254–260; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S017; DATE: 2008-10; WORK: Autonomy Levels for Unmanned Systems (ALFUS) Framework, Volume I: Terminology; VERSION: NIST Special Publication 1011-I-2.0, Version 2.0; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Contextual descriptions of agent autonomy, with ALFUS organising mission/environment/human-independence terminology.
Development
Use a task-relative vector or equivalent description, not a ladder whose highest level is automatically preferable.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Use a task-relative vector or equivalent description, not a ladder whose highest level is automatically preferable.; OPERATION: Describe separately what the agent chooses, what resources and duration it controls, what it can learn, and the conditions under which people intervene; relate these to task/environment difficulty.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A short capability-and-permission description is enough when only one decision is delegated; no multi-axis scoring dashboard is obligatory.
Problem
A single autonomy score conflates action selection, goal choice, persistence, adaptation and freedom from direct intervention.
Mechanism
Describe separately what the agent chooses, what resources and duration it controls, what it can learn, and the conditions under which people intervene; relate these to task/environment difficulty.
Trigger and context
Comparing or delegating different degrees of autonomy.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Delegator and capability evaluator
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator and capability evaluator; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Social ability is required by some definitions but does not mean every task needs negotiation; user autonomy remains distinct. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator and capability evaluator. Explicit task context and independently described capability and authorisation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Two systems with different goal, action and oversight powers are not treated as equivalent merely because both are called autonomous.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
A short capability-and-permission description is enough when only one decision is delegated; no multi-axis scoring dashboard is obligatory.

EAA-003 — Computational grounding of mental-state vocabulary (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S010; DATE: 1993; WORK: Agent-oriented programming; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 60, 51–92; full journal scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Mental-state programming and BDI logic with explicitly discussed abstraction/interpreter gaps.
Development
Retain useful intentional abstractions but demand an explicit abstraction-to-implementation relation for technical claims.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain useful intentional abstractions but demand an explicit abstraction-to-implementation relation for technical claims.; OPERATION: Link each engineering use of belief, desire, intention or commitment to a representation, transition, consumer and observable behaviour; distinguish an explanatory stance from executable semantics.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use ordinary state, task and action names when intentional vocabulary adds no predictive or engineering value.
Problem
Words such as belief and intention can create apparent guarantees without any corresponding state transition or decision rule.
Mechanism
Link each engineering use of belief, desire, intention or commitment to a representation, transition, consumer and observable behaviour; distinguish an explanatory stance from executable semantics.
Trigger and context
Claims of BDI, persistent intention, belief revision or communicative understanding.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Accessible implementation semantics or an explicitly scoped abstract specification.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

EAA-004 — Capability is not delegated permission (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Human control over adaptive assistants, including action-specific suggestion versus execution thresholds.
Development
Preserve meaningful control at consequential decisions, not maximally frequent consent prompts or blanket autonomy.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Preserve meaningful control at consequential decisions, not maximally frequent consent prompts or blanket autonomy.; OPERATION: Keep the means the system can execute separate from what the delegator has authorised; make consequential scope boundaries usable at action selection and dispatch.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No additional permission mechanism is needed for an already constrained, harmless action entirely within the declared task; a fixed capability sandbox may suffice.
Problem
A tool’s availability or a plan’s feasibility can be mistaken for permission to act by every available means.
Mechanism
Keep the means the system can execute separate from what the delegator has authorised; make consequential scope boundaries usable at action selection and dispatch.
Trigger and context
Actions affect other people, user resources, external accounts, physical systems or protected information.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Delegator, executor and affected participant
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Delegator, executor and affected participant; BOUNDARY: This is a research/evaluation criterion or rejected inference; it does not itself confer operative authority. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: Delegator, executor and affected participant. A source of task authority, an interpretable scope and an actuator that can respect limitations.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
An out-of-scope action is refused or referred rather than legitimised by a high success probability.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
No additional permission mechanism is needed for an already constrained, harmless action entirely within the declared task; a fixed capability sandbox may suffice.
Historical disposition
STRONGLY_RETAINED
Failure modes
Granular confirmations can overwhelm people; user permission alone may not settle rights or safety of affected third parties.; Introduced cost or harm: Friction, delayed action and permission-management complexity.
Criticism

Contrary evidence

Assumption sensitivity
Authority is a normative/task relation, not a learned affordance score. Specific legal obligations are not derived here.
Unresolved questions
What evidence establishes permission for this means, as distinct from capability and a broad task request?; Authority is a normative/task relation, not a learned affordance score. Specific legal obligations are not derived here.

EAA-005 — Observation, stored belief and world state remain distinct (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S021; DATE: undated author manuscript (workshop-era; exact year unverified); WORK: Electric Elves: What Went Wrong and Why; VERSION: Six-page author manuscript; distinct from the later 2008 AI Magazine article; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
An observation-conditioned state estimate; early office assistance initially inferred availability from a poor proxy.
Development
Preserve provenance and actionable uncertainty; prefer direct feedback where it supplies sufficient discrimination.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Preserve provenance and actionable uncertainty; prefer direct feedback where it supplies sufficient discrimination.; OPERATION: Retain the relevant observation source and interpretation; update a state estimate without equating observation, inference and reality. Gather discriminating evidence when the distinction changes action.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a directly validated signal without a separate belief database when ambiguity cannot affect the decision.
Problem
A report or sensor reading may be false, ambiguous or misinterpreted, yet become treated as an established world fact.
Mechanism
Retain the relevant observation source and interpretation; update a state estimate without equating observation, inference and reality. Gather discriminating evidence when the distinction changes action.
Trigger and context
Noisy sensors, tool output, inferred availability, delayed messages or conflicting reports.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Authority boundary

Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: State estimator and action selector. An observation interface and a way to distinguish source reports from inferred state.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Decision-relevant uncertainty or disagreement survives into the action decision instead of being silently erased.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
Use a directly validated signal without a separate belief database when ambiguity cannot affect the decision.

EAA-006 — Decision-relevant freshness and change detection (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S015; DATE: 1991; WORK: Commitment and Effectiveness of Situated Agents; VERSION: IJCAI 1991 pp.82–88; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Reactive context checks and reconsideration in changing environments, alongside asynchronous executive state.
Development
Use selective event- or risk-triggered refresh rather than permanent global resensing.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Use selective event- or risk-triggered refresh rather than permanent global resensing.; OPERATION: Associate consequential observations and decisions with their validity conditions; re-observe or invalidate them when elapsed time, events or concurrent actions can change preconditions.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Reuse a state estimate without refresh when the relevant variables are stable or the action is insensitive to change.
Problem
A correct plan can be based on a state that ceased to hold while the agent reasoned or waited.
Mechanism
Associate consequential observations and decisions with their validity conditions; re-observe or invalidate them when elapsed time, events or concurrent actions can change preconditions.
Trigger and context
Dynamic environments, delayed tool responses, long deliberation or shared mutable objects.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer

Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Executor and state-estimation process; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: Executor and state-estimation process. A model of which changes matter, access to a current signal when needed and an actionable response to invalidation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
An action whose supporting state is stale is rechecked, replaced or withheld before preventable misuse.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
Reuse a state estimate without refresh when the relevant variables are stable or the action is insensitive to change.

EAA-007 — Adequate uncertainty representation rather than a complete world model (ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S005; DATE: 1991; WORK: Intelligence without representation; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 47, 139–159; author reformatted copy; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S012; DATE: 1998; WORK: Planning and Acting in Partially Observable Stochastic Domains; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 101, 99–134; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Belief-state planning under a specified partial-observation model, contrasted with direct environmental coupling.
Development
Retain representation adequacy as a conditional requirement, not completeness or a mandatory probability formalism.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain representation adequacy as a conditional requirement, not completeness or a mandatory probability formalism.; OPERATION: Use history, a sufficient statistic, a belief distribution or a smaller task-relevant state estimate only to the extent needed for action discrimination.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A reactive mapping is sufficient when observations already determine an adequate action; approximate state may dominate exact belief planning.
Problem
Current observations may alias states that require different actions, while a full model is computationally unattainable.
Mechanism
Use history, a sufficient statistic, a belief distribution or a smaller task-relevant state estimate only to the extent needed for action discrimination.
Trigger and context
Partial observability, uncertain transitions or delayed effects make an observation-only policy inadequate.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-005; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
State estimator and planner
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: State estimator and planner; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Human reports and tool returns are observations with distinct trust and authority roles; assistance can be an information action. Consumer and prerequisite: State estimator and planner. A useful transition/observation abstraction and enough information or learning to maintain it.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
The estimator distinguishes action-relevant alternatives and exposes material model uncertainty.

Development

Historical origin

Original form

Development

EAA-011 — Achievement and maintenance commitments are not interchangeable (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S051; DATE: 2012-07-24; WORK: Maintenance Goals in Intelligent Agents; VERSION: Accepted author manuscript; final Computational Intelligence 30(1), 71–114 issue 2014; DOI 10.1111/coin.12000; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Achievement goals contrasted with continuing maintenance; reactive restoration and predictive prevention.
Development
Choose reactive or proactive maintenance using model quality, lead time and violation cost, not a universal preference for anticipation.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Choose reactive or proactive maintenance using model quality, lead time and violation cost, not a universal preference for anticipation.; OPERATION: Distinguish reaching a goal from maintaining a condition; retain monitoring after restoration where required, using predictive intervention only when the prediction is sufficiently credible.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A one-shot achievement criterion suffices for genuinely terminal tasks; reactive restoration may be cheaper than prediction.
Problem
Restoring a condition once can be mistaken for satisfying a duty to keep it true.
Mechanism

EAA-012 — Concurrent-goal interference protection (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin

Original form
Concurrent goal plans summarised by possible/definite conditions and effects in a known hierarchy.
Development
Preserve interference discrimination while selecting the cheapest adequate analysis, isolation or scheduling mechanism.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Preserve interference discrimination while selecting the cheapest adequate analysis, isolation or scheduling mechanism.; OPERATION: Use task-relevant condition/effect/resource summaries or another adequate conflict test to prevent mutually destructive concurrent commitments and to revise summaries as execution proceeds.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No conflict machinery is necessary for a single isolated goal or demonstrably independent actions; serialisation can be adequate if affordable.
Problem
Individually valid intentions may consume the same resource or negate each other’s preconditions.
Mechanism

Trigger and context
Parallel goals or plans share mutable conditions, resources or deadlines.

EAA-014 — Economical reconsideration scheduling (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin

EAA-015 — Retirement of obsolete, unreachable or exhausted goals (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S052; DATE: 1990-05-01; WORK: On Acting Together; VERSION: SRI Technical Note 485, submitted to AAAI 1990; scanned author report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S051; DATE: 2012-07-24; WORK: Maintenance Goals in Intelligent Agents; VERSION: Accepted author manuscript; final Computational Intelligence 30(1), 71–114 issue 2014; DOI 10.1111/coin.12000; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S020; DATE: 2002; WORK: Towards Adjustable Autonomy for the Real World; VERSION: JAIR 17, 171–228; author manuscript paginated 1–58; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Persistent-goal termination on achievement, impossibility or loss of relevance; later resource-sensitive failure examples.
Development
Termination is evidence- and authority-sensitive, with explicit uncertainty rather than a success claim after giving up.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Termination is evidence- and authority-sensitive, with explicit uncertainty rather than a success claim after giving up.; OPERATION: Use relevance, feasibility, progress and budget evidence to suspend or retire pursuit; distinguish failure of one plan from impossibility of the goal and report material non-fulfilment.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Continue without a new retirement procedure when existing bounded execution already enforces the stop condition.
Problem
An agent may keep retrying an impossible task, consume resources or continue after the user’s purpose changes.
Mechanism
Use relevance, feasibility, progress and budget evidence to suspend or retire pursuit; distinguish failure of one plan from impossibility of the goal and report material non-fulfilment.
Trigger and context
Changed task, repeated unproductive attempts, exhausted resources or established infeasibility.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A legitimate goal source, evidence of progress/failure and an executable stop or handback path.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Goal manager and delegator
Authority boundary

Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A legitimate goal source, evidence of progress/failure and an executable stop or handback path.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Clarify changed task meaning and report abandonment/non-fulfilment to the legitimate consumer where consequential. Consumer and prerequisite: Goal manager and delegator. A legitimate goal source, evidence of progress/failure and an executable stop or handback path.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
Continue without a new retirement procedure when existing bounded execution already enforces the stop condition.

EAA-018 — Executable BDI and plan-library interpretation (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S008; DATE: 1991-02; WORK: Modeling Rational Agents within a BDI-Architecture; VERSION: Australian AI Institute Technical Note 14; KR 1991 antecedent; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S009; DATE: 1995; WORK: BDI Agents: From Theory to Practice; VERSION: ICMAS 1995 pp.312–319; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
BDI abstract interpreters and practical plan-library approximations.
Development
Retain BDI as a family of practical choices with documented abstraction gaps, not a guarantee produced by naming three stores.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain BDI as a family of practical choices with documented abstraction gaps, not a guarantee produced by naming three stores.; OPERATION: Maintain relevant beliefs, goals/events and adopted intentions; select applicable library plans and update or drop them through explicit interpreter rules.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A simpler task state machine or direct workflow can suffice when intention filtering and alternative plans add no value.
Problem
Generic reasoning may be too expensive, while a library of context-sensitive plans needs principled goal selection and commitment.
Mechanism
Maintain relevant beliefs, goals/events and adopted intentions; select applicable library plans and update or drop them through explicit interpreter rules.
Trigger and context

EAA-021 — Ground actions in executable capabilities and affordances (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S011; DATE: 1971; WORK: STRIPS: A New Approach to the Application of Theorem Proving to Problem Solving; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 2, 189–208; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S045; DATE: 1996; WORK: Planning-Based Control of Software Agents; VERSION: AIPS 1996 proceedings, pp.268–274; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S049; DATE: 2022-08-16; WORK: Do As I Can, Not As I Say: Grounding Language in Robotic Affordances; VERSION: arXiv:2204.01691v2; initial submission April 2022; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form

Development

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Ground both action identity and context-sensitive feasibility while retaining separate authority and effect checks.; OPERATION: Map proposed actions to actual capability interfaces and task-relevant feasibility estimates; reject invented actions and distinguish a skill’s availability from its likely success.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A fixed typed action set is enough where availability and effects are already established; learned affordance scoring is not mandatory.
Problem
A plan may name an action the executor cannot perform, or assign it an effect unsupported by the available skill.
Mechanism

Trigger and context
Planning with tools, robots or dynamically available capabilities.

A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Every dispatched action denotes an available capability with interpretable parameters and a justified relation to the plan.

EAA-022 — Recheck material action conditions at dispatch (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S011; DATE: 1971; WORK: STRIPS: A New Approach to the Application of Theorem Proving to Problem Solving; VERSION: Artificial Intelligence 2, 189–208; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S039; DATE: 2026-08-23; WORK: Physical Agentic AI: An Architecture for Orchestrating a Robot Crew with LLMs; VERSION: arXiv:2608.22657v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Context-conditioned reactive methods and action applicability, later native runtime enforcement of generated plans.
Development
Retain action-time validation with honest sensing limits, not a universal gate that supposedly eliminates every unknown risk.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain action-time validation with honest sensing limits, not a universal gate that supposedly eliminates every unknown risk.; OPERATION: At the consequential dispatch boundary, check the still-relevant feasibility, state and delegated constraints; reject, refresh or refer invalid requests rather than relying only on earlier planning.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use existing type/actuator checks or an atomic constrained operation when these already establish the required conditions.
Problem
A plan valid when selected can become invalid before execution, or a generated command can bypass relevant conditions.
Mechanism
At the consequential dispatch boundary, check the still-relevant feasibility, state and delegated constraints; reject, refresh or refer invalid requests rather than relying only on earlier planning.
Trigger and context
Mutable conditions, learned/generated plans, significant delays or consequential actions.
Preconditions

EAA-023 — Distinguish issuance, acknowledgement and established effect (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Execution monitors and result/state checks distinguish intended effects from command issue.
Development
Retain evidence-proportionate completion semantics and explicitly unresolved outcomes, not ritual double-checking of every operation.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain evidence-proportionate completion semantics and explicitly unresolved outcomes, not ritual double-checking of every operation.; OPERATION: Interpret acknowledgement according to its actual semantics; observe the task-relevant effect or retain uncertainty when it cannot be established, and use that result in subsequent action and reporting.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A trustworthy atomic operation with a sufficient return contract may establish the effect directly; do not require redundant observation.
Problem
A sent command or success-looking tool response can be mistaken for the intended external change.
Mechanism
Interpret acknowledgement according to its actual semantics; observe the task-relevant effect or retain uncertainty when it cannot be established, and use that result in subsequent action and reporting.
Trigger and context
Actions with asynchronous, fallible, delayed or externally mediated effects.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A defined desired effect, a credible observation or return contract and a consumer of the result.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S016; DATE: 1987; WORK: An Investigation into Reactive Planning in Complex Domains; VERSION: AAAI 1987 pp.202–206; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S030; DATE: 2023; WORK: ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models; VERSION: arXiv:2210.03629v3; initial submission 2022; ICLR 2023 work; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Alternative reactive methods, monitoring, contingency choice and plan revision.
Development

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain failure-sensitive repair with explicit stopping and escalation, not retry count as a proxy for persistence or competence.; OPERATION: Use observed failure to choose an applicable alternative, repair the plan or hand back; bound repetition by progress evidence, remaining resources and irreversible consequences.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a known safe retry for a transient failure when its conditions are established; stop directly when no useful recovery exists.
Problem

EAA-025 — Irreversibility and retry/compensation boundary (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Action-specific human control considering reversibility, combined analytically with acknowledgement ambiguity.
Development
Retain consequence-sensitive execution and recovery as analytical composition of the sources, not a newly claimed native transaction theory.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain consequence-sensitive execution and recovery as analytical composition of the sources, not a newly claimed native transaction theory.; OPERATION: Distinguish reversible, compensable, idempotent and irreversible actions where relevant; choose confirmation, evidence and recovery proportional to the actual effect, not just the command interface.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A harmless idempotent action may be retried without additional ceremony when that property is established.
Problem
Retrying an unobserved action can duplicate an effect, while “undo” can be falsely treated as erasing harm.
Mechanism
Distinguish reversible, compensable, idempotent and irreversible actions where relevant; choose confirmation, evidence and recovery proportional to the actual effect, not just the command interface.
Trigger and context
External messages, resource spending, physical movement, destructive updates or uncertain action completion.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Known effect semantics, credible completion evidence and authority for any compensating action.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Executor and authorising participant
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Executor and authorising participant; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Known effect semantics, credible completion evidence and authority for any compensating action.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Report material failure or unresolved effects; seek permission for genuine task changes and provide feasible handback. Consumer and prerequisite: Executor and authorising participant. Known effect semantics, credible completion evidence and authority for any compensating action.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
An uncertain irreversible effect does not trigger a blind duplicate; compensation is described as a new action with its own residual consequences.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
A harmless idempotent action may be retried without additional ceremony when that property is established.
Historical disposition
RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM
Failure modes
The agent tradition does not supply a universal distributed-transaction guarantee; a user-approved action can still harm third parties.; Introduced cost or harm: Conservative delays, confirmation burden and incomplete restoration.
Criticism
SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: The agent tradition does not supply a universal distributed-transaction guarantee; a user-approved action can still harm third parties.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C006; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S043; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.
Contrary evidence
STATEMENT: The agent tradition does not supply a universal distributed-transaction guarantee; a user-approved action can still harm third parties.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S043; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.
Assumption sensitivity
Idempotence and reversibility must be demonstrated for the operation and environment; neither follows from a button labelled undo.
Unresolved questions
What happens if this command already took effect, and can the proposed recovery actually restore what matters?; Idempotence and reversibility must be demonstrated for the operation and environment; neither follows from a button labelled undo.
Property versus ceremony
CEREMONY_ID: EAA-CE025; PROPERTY_ID: EAA-025; ARTEFACT_RITUAL_OR_BRAND: Action-specific human control considering reversibility, combined analytically with acknowledgement ambiguity.; PROTECTED_PROPERTY_AND_FAILURE: Retrying an unobserved action can duplicate an effect, while “undo” can be falsely treated as erasing harm.; CONSUMER: Executor and authorising participant; PREREQUISITES: Known effect semantics, credible completion evidence and authority for any compensating action.; MINIMAL_ADEQUATE_ALTERNATIVE: A harmless idempotent action may be retried without additional ceremony when that property is established.; TRIGGER_FOR_FULLER_FORM: External messages, resource spending, physical movement, destructive updates or uncertain action completion.; COSTS_OF_SIMPLIFICATION: Omitting this mechanism can reintroduce the specific failure: Retrying an unobserved action can duplicate an effect, while “undo” can be falsely treated as erasing harm. Simplification is acceptable only while the cheaper path still establishes the relevant criterion.; OMISSION_OR_RETIREMENT_CONDITIONS: Omit or retire a dedicated implementation when this trigger no longer applies, its consumer disappears, or an existing cheaper mechanism demonstrably establishes the same criterion. Do not erase relevant genealogy or unresolved effects. Specific non-trigger: A harmless idempotent action may be retried without additional ceremony when that property is established.; CONTROL_COST: Conservative delays, confirmation burden and incomplete restoration.; ANTI_CEREMONY_BOUNDARY: The presence of an artefact, module, prompt or meeting is insufficient. The functional evidence is: An uncertain irreversible effect does not trigger a blind duplicate; compensation is described as a new action with its own residual consequences.
Evidence profile at historical freeze

They do not identify a universal causal payoff for the mature composed property.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; FIELD_OR_DEPLOYMENT_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: LIMITED_AUTHOR_REPORTED; BASIS: Field/robot programme observations support feasibility or concrete failure modes under their actual settings. Shared programmes are not independent replications and not every cited robot experiment is uncontrolled deployment.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; INDEPENDENT_REPLICATION: STRENGTH: NOT_ESTABLISHED_FOR_THIS_GENERAL_PROPERTY; BASIS: The corpus distinguishes repeated trials from independent replications. Shared datasets/programmes and author follow-ups are registered; no general-property replication rate is invented.; CONTRARY_EVIDENCE: STRENGTH: MATERIAL_SCOPE_OR_FAILURE_LIMIT; BASIS: The agent tradition does not supply a universal distributed-transaction guarantee; a user-approved action can still harm third parties.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S022; EAA-S043; TRANSFERABILITY: STRENGTH: CONDITIONAL_NEW_CLAIM; BASIS: Idempotence and reversibility must be demonstrated for the operation and environment; neither follows from a button labelled undo. Target evidence must establish the relevant observability, authority, timing, effect and evaluation conditions.; SYNTHESIS_STATUS: STRENGTH: RESEARCHER_INTERPRETATION; BASIS: Retain consequence-sensitive execution and recovery as analytical composition of the sources, not a newly claimed native transaction theory.

EAA-026 — Explicit time and resource envelope (STRONGLY_RETAINED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S013; DATE: 1955-02; WORK: A Behavioral Model of Rational Choice; VERSION: Quarterly Journal of Economics 69(1), 99–118; CMU archival scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S014; DATE: 1995-05; WORK: Provably Bounded-Optimal Agents; VERSION: JAIR 2, 575–609; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Aspiration/resource-relative reasoning and machine/environment-relative bounded optimality.
Development
Retain explicit whole-task constraints while comparing quality and costs separately instead of collapsing them into one misleading score.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain explicit whole-task constraints while comparing quality and costs separately instead of collapsing them into one misleading score.; OPERATION: Include deliberation, sensing, retries and coordination in the operative resource envelope; let remaining resources constrain action and continuation decisions.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A simple fixed bound suffices for predictable bounded tasks; no optimiser is required merely to enforce a cap.
Problem
An apparently improving decision process can exceed the task’s time, compute, tool or attention budget.
Mechanism
Include deliberation, sensing, retries and coordination in the operative resource envelope; let remaining resources constrain action and continuation decisions.
Trigger and context
Finite deadlines, paid computation/tools, limited energy or human attention.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Measurable or conservatively bounded costs, a decision horizon and an executable response to exhaustion.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Deliberation scheduler, delegator and evaluator
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Deliberation scheduler, delegator and evaluator; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Measurable or conservatively bounded costs, a decision horizon and an executable response to exhaustion.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Consultation consumes another actor’s time and may fail to receive a reply; attention is not free computation. Consumer and prerequisite: Deliberation scheduler, delegator and evaluator. Measurable or conservatively bounded costs, a decision horizon and an executable response to exhaustion.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
The agent does not treat reasoning or assistance as free, and can stop or fall back before exceeding the declared envelope.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
A simple fixed bound suffices for predictable bounded tasks; no optimiser is required merely to enforce a cap.
Historical disposition
STRONGLY_RETAINED
Failure modes
Budget estimates may omit setup, human repair or retained infrastructure; a cheaper failed action may be worse overall.; Introduced cost or harm: Metering overhead and underuse of resources if limits are too conservative.
Criticism
SOURCE_GROUNDED_OBJECTION: Budget estimates may omit setup, human repair or retained infrastructure; a cheaper failed action may be worse overall.; CRITICISM_IDS: EAA-C003; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S035; ANALYTICAL_ADVERSARIAL_TEST: Challenge the payoff by the cheaper path, failed prerequisites, introduced cost and target-specific contrary evidence; do not count the mechanism as present merely because its artefact exists.
Contrary evidence
STATEMENT: Budget estimates may omit setup, human repair or retained infrastructure; a cheaper failed action may be worse overall.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S035; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.
Assumption sensitivity
Bounded optimality is machine/environment relative; cost weights are task commitments, not universal constants.
Unresolved questions
Which resources can this task consume, including reasoning and repair, and what enforces the limit?; Bounded optimality is machine/environment relative; cost weights are task commitments, not universal constants.
Property versus ceremony

The functional evidence is: The agent does not treat reasoning or assistance as free, and can stop or fall back before exceeding the declared envelope.

EAA-027 — Anytime response or adequate deadline fallback (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S014; DATE: 1995-05; WORK: Provably Bounded-Optimal Agents; VERSION: JAIR 2, 575–609; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S046; DATE: 1995; WORK: Experiences with an Architecture for Intelligent, Reactive Agents; VERSION: ATAL 1995 workshop author copy; 17 pages; distinct later journal version; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Anytime/deadline-sensitive decision procedures and bounded reactive execution.
Development
Retain deadline adequacy, with anytime methods as one option rather than a universal architecture.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain deadline adequacy, with anytime methods as one option rather than a universal architecture.; OPERATION: Provide an adequate available decision or fallback by the relevant deadline; improve it while time remains only where the algorithm and execution context support interruption.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A fast fixed policy can dominate an anytime solver; a batch answer is fine when no action deadline exists.
Problem
A solver may produce an excellent answer only after the decision opportunity has disappeared.
Mechanism
Provide an adequate available decision or fallback by the relevant deadline; improve it while time remains only where the algorithm and execution context support interruption.
Trigger and context
Hard or soft action deadlines and variable computation time.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A usable intermediate answer or fallback, an interruption mechanism and known timing obligations.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Runtime scheduler and executor
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Runtime scheduler and executor; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A usable intermediate answer or fallback, an interruption mechanism and known timing obligations.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Consultation consumes another actor’s time and may fail to receive a reply; attention is not free computation. Consumer and prerequisite: Runtime scheduler and executor. A usable intermediate answer or fallback, an interruption mechanism and known timing obligations.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
The system can produce or enact an adequate timely response rather than waiting indefinitely for an ideal result.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
A fast fixed policy can dominate an anytime solver; a batch answer is fine when no action deadline exists.
Historical disposition
CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
Failure modes
Calling a module reactive or requesting a frequency does not make it hard real-time; some algorithms have no useful intermediate result.; Introduced cost or harm: Inferior early decisions and engineering overhead for interruptible computation.
Criticism

STATEMENT: Calling a module reactive or requesting a frequency does not make it hard real-time; some algorithms have no useful intermediate result.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S046; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.
Assumption sensitivity
Worst-case execution time and safe interruption may need separate evidence; simulated bounded optimality is not real-time certification.
Unresolved questions
What valid response exists when the available thinking time expires?; Worst-case execution time and safe interruption may need separate evidence; simulated bounded optimality is not real-time certification.

EAA-028 — Metareasoning and value of computation (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S006; DATE: 1988; WORK: Plans and Resource-Bounded Practical Reasoning; VERSION: Computational Intelligence 4, 349–355; 30-page author-formatted manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S013; DATE: 1955-02; WORK: A Behavioral Model of Rational Choice; VERSION: Quarterly Journal of Economics 69(1), 99–118; CMU archival scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S014; DATE: 1995-05; WORK: Provably Bounded-Optimal Agents; VERSION: JAIR 2, 575–609; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Selection among feasible decision procedures based on computation quality and cost.
Development
Retain operational stopping/selection discipline; allow heuristics where a full metalevel calculation is unjustified.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain operational stopping/selection discipline; allow heuristics where a full metalevel calculation is unjustified.; OPERATION: Choose between further computation, acting, observing, consulting or terminating by an adequate estimate of expected improvement net of relevant delay and resource cost.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A fixed cheap policy or simple stopping rule suffices where estimating the value of computation would cost more than it saves.
Problem
Reasoning about an action can improve it, but can also cost more than the improvement or make its premises stale.
Mechanism
Choose between further computation, acting, observing, consulting or terminating by an adequate estimate of expected improvement net of relevant delay and resource cost.
Trigger and context
Alternative reasoning procedures, variable difficulty or changing action windows.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Available alternative actions/computations, interpretable costs and an estimate useful enough to influence selection.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Deliberation scheduler
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Deliberation scheduler; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Available alternative actions/computations, interpretable costs and an estimate useful enough to influence selection.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Consultation consumes another actor’s time and may fail to receive a reply; attention is not free computation. Consumer and prerequisite: Deliberation scheduler. Available alternative actions/computations, interpretable costs and an estimate useful enough to influence selection.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Further reasoning has a stated opportunity-cost justification rather than being invoked as a universal virtue.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
A fixed cheap policy or simple stopping rule suffices where estimating the value of computation would cost more than it saves.
Historical disposition
CONTEXT_DEPENDENT

Contrary evidence
STATEMENT: Metareasoning can recurse into another expensive optimisation; bounded-optimal results assume a specified machine and environment.; SOURCE_IDS: EAA-S015; EAA-S035; IS_GENERAL_REFUTATION: false; LIMIT: A counterexample to a universal claim is not a population failure-rate estimate; non-conforming implementation failures do not logically refute a theorem under its assumptions.
Assumption sensitivity
Expected decision improvement is uncertain and must not be mistaken for guaranteed benefit.
Unresolved questions
What evidence makes another unit of reasoning better than the best currently available action?; Expected decision improvement is uncertain and must not be mistaken for guaranteed benefit.

Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form

Development

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain provenance-aware selective memory, not the slogan that longer context or more recollection necessarily improves agency.; OPERATION: Retain the origin and evidential status of consequential experience; distinguish observed outcomes, user correction and generated interpretation, and avoid promoting unverified self-reports to fact.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Omit persistent memory when the task does not benefit from it; keep a small verified example set rather than an indiscriminate transcript archive.
Problem
An agent can store its own mistaken interpretation as experience and then use that memory as confirmation.
Mechanism

EAA-031 — Selective retrieval, forgetting and memory retirement (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S050; DATE: 2023-08-06; WORK: Generative Agents: Interactive Simulacra of Human Behavior; VERSION: arXiv:2304.03442v2; UIST 2023 DOI 10.1145/3586183.3606763; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Bounded exemplar deletion, limited reflection buffers and relevance-based retrieval.
Development
Retain purposeful memory selection with explicit loss costs rather than maximising memory or forgetting uniformly.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain purposeful memory selection with explicit loss costs rather than maximising memory or forgetting uniformly.; OPERATION: Retrieve memory according to task relevance and validity; bound retention and retire or down-weight entries when their purpose, authority or applicability expires.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No persistent store or a short task-local buffer can be sufficient; deletion is appropriate when no legitimate future consumer remains.
Problem
Accumulating all experience can increase cost, expose private information and amplify irrelevant or obsolete episodes.
Mechanism

EAA-032 — Adaptation with continuity checks (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S026; DATE: 2017-01-25; WORK: Overcoming catastrophic forgetting in neural networks; VERSION: arXiv:1612.00796v2; initial submission December 2016; distinct PNAS edition; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S053; DATE: 2016; WORK: Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents; VERSION: AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Selective protection of previously important parameters in sequential-task learning.
Development
Retain continuity as an evaluation obligation with alternative mechanisms, not a universal mandate for parameter regularisation.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain continuity as an evaluation obligation with alternative mechanisms, not a universal mandate for parameter regularisation.; OPERATION: Evaluate changes against both new-task benefit and relevant retained capabilities/constraints; use an appropriate retention, rehearsal, modularity or change-limiting method where it earns its cost.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Keep a fixed adequate policy when adaptation offers no demonstrated value; independent task modules may avoid unnecessary shared updates.
Problem
Learning a new task can degrade earlier competence or alter behaviour that other actors still rely upon.
Mechanism
Evaluate changes against both new-task benefit and relevant retained capabilities/constraints; use an appropriate retention, rehearsal, modularity or change-limiting method where it earns its cost.
Trigger and context

Historical origin

Original form
Exploration under learning assumptions and later demonstrations/constraints intended to reduce unsafe actions.
Development
Retain bounded protected exploration, not “safe learning” as a blanket guarantee from a reward penalty or demonstration set.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain bounded protected exploration, not “safe learning” as a blanket guarantee from a reward penalty or demonstration set.; OPERATION: Select exploration within protected or sufficiently constrained settings; distinguish learning a risk estimate from actually enforcing a constraint, and test residual violations as well as reward.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a known adequate policy, demonstrations or simulation when live exploration is unnecessary or not authorised.
Problem
Exploration that improves a policy can impose unacceptable costs before learning succeeds.
Mechanism
Select exploration within protected or sufficiently constrained settings; distinguish learning a risk estimate from actually enforcing a constraint, and test residual violations as well as reward.
Trigger and context
Novel actions, uncertain transitions or adaptive policies with consequential external effects.

EAA-036 — Communication receipt, acceptance and action are separate (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S028; DATE: 2007; WORK: On the Formal Semantics of Speech-Act Based Communication in an Agent-Oriented Programming Language; VERSION: JAIR 29, 221–267; arXiv upload 2011; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S020; DATE: 2002; WORK: Towards Adjustable Autonomy for the Real World; VERSION: JAIR 17, 171–228; author manuscript paginated 1–58; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Speech acts interpreted through receive, social-acceptance and state-update rules.
Development
Retain consequential communication state distinctions without requiring a universal speech-act language or complete collective theory.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain consequential communication state distinctions without requiring a universal speech-act language or complete collective theory.; OPERATION: Distinguish transmission, receipt, interpretation, acceptance and any resulting commitment/effect; seek only the acknowledgements needed for the consequential coordination decision.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No acknowledgement is needed for a genuinely non-critical broadcast; an existing reliable protocol may already establish sufficient receipt semantics.
Problem
Sending a request can be mistaken for shared belief, accepted commitment or completed action by the recipient.
Mechanism
Distinguish transmission, receipt, interpretation, acceptance and any resulting commitment/effect; seek only the acknowledgements needed for the consequential coordination decision.
Trigger and context
Delegation, information exchange, handback or reliance on another actor’s action.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Identified participants, applicable message semantics and a decision that consumes the response.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Sender, recipient and coordinating decision process
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Sender, recipient and coordinating decision process; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Identified participants, applicable message semantics and a decision that consumes the response.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: Sender, recipient and coordinating decision process. Identified participants, applicable message semantics and a decision that consumes the response.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
The sender does not infer an accepted goal or external effect merely from having emitted a message.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
No acknowledgement is needed for a genuinely non-critical broadcast; an existing reliable protocol may already establish sufficient receipt semantics.
Historical disposition
RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM

EAA-037 — Cost-aware clarification and mixed initiative (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S044; DATE: 1993; WORK: Learning Interface Agents; VERSION: AAAI 1993, pp.459–465; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Mixed-initiative principles, confidence thresholds and dialogue choices.
Development
Retain assistance as a choice inside the task, not a mandatory interview or an assumption that greater initiative is better.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain assistance as a choice inside the task, not a mandatory interview or an assumption that greater initiative is better.; OPERATION: Compare acting, suggesting, asking and doing less using uncertainty, consequence, attention and expected response; let humans revise task meaning and terminate unwanted assistance.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use direct manipulation or a harmless established default when clarification adds no value.
Problem
Initiative taken on ambiguous preferences can be wrong, while asking about everything burdens the user.
Mechanism
Compare acting, suggesting, asking and doing less using uncertainty, consequence, attention and expected response; let humans revise task meaning and terminate unwanted assistance.
Trigger and context
A consequential ambiguity or an opportunity for useful assistance not fully determined by the current task.
Preconditions

A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
