EAA-038 — Conditional transfer of control (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S020; DATE: 2002; WORK: Towards Adjustable Autonomy for the Real World; VERSION: JAIR 17, 171–228; author manuscript paginated 1–58; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S037; DATE: 2026-05; WORK: A Conceptual Framework for Shared Autonomy; VERSION: AAMAS 2026 extended abstract, pp.3453–3455; DOI 10.65109/QRXH6744; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Transfer-of-control strategies with response probabilities and delay/coordination costs.
Development
Retain contingent transfer strategies, including non-response, not “human in the loop” as a self-sufficient assurance claim.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain contingent transfer strategies, including non-response, not “human in the loop” as a self-sufficient assurance claim.; OPERATION: Choose who should make a particular decision, when to transfer it and what to do if the recipient does not respond; account for decision quality, delay and coordination consequences.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Keep control where it already yields an adequate timely decision; a fixed allocation suffices for stable simple tasks.
Problem
Neither permanent agent independence nor permanent human control handles every changing decision situation.
Mechanism
Choose who should make a particular decision, when to transfer it and what to do if the recipient does not respond; account for decision quality, delay and coordination consequences.
Trigger and context
A decision exceeds agent competence, information or delegated scope, or a human can materially improve it.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A legitimate recipient, understood authority, communication and an executable timeout/continuation policy.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-036; EAA-039; EAA-040; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer

Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. A legitimate recipient, understood authority, communication and an executable timeout/continuation policy.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: Agent, delegator and receiving human/agent. A legitimate recipient, understood authority, communication and an executable timeout/continuation policy.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Control transfer has a defined recipient and follow-through rather than ending in an unanswered request or assumed acceptance.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
Keep control where it already yields an adequate timely decision; a fixed allocation suffices for stable simple tasks.

EAA-039 — Human readiness and safe handback conditions (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S023; DATE: 1983; WORK: Ironies of Automation; VERSION: Automatica 19(6), 775–779; original journal scan; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S020; DATE: 2002; WORK: Towards Adjustable Autonomy for the Real World; VERSION: JAIR 17, 171–228; author manuscript paginated 1–58; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Automation’s skill/vigilance problem and later unanswered or difficult control transfers.
Development
Retain readiness and latency as actual prerequisites for human oversight, not a universal rescue assumption.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain readiness and latency as actual prerequisites for human oversight, not a universal rescue assumption.; OPERATION: Provide the consequential state and options needed for a feasible takeover; account for recognition and response time, and establish what the system does until control is genuinely assumed.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: An immediate direct control interface may suffice for a simple visible action; avoid elaborate handover forms without a consumer.
Problem
A nominal override can be unusable if the person lacks current context, time, skill or actual control.
Mechanism
Provide the consequential state and options needed for a feasible takeover; account for recognition and response time, and establish what the system does until control is genuinely assumed.
Trigger and context
Time-sensitive escalation, automation failure or tasks that people rarely perform manually.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A reachable capable participant, sufficient time and a physically/operationally feasible holding or fallback state.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Oversight designer and receiving participant
Authority boundary

EAA-040 — Operational revocation and interruption (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S019; DATE: 1999; WORK: Principles of Mixed-Initiative User Interfaces; VERSION: CHI 1999; author manuscript; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Meaningful direct termination/override and actual ground interruption of an autonomous execution.
Development
Retain effective, consequence-sensitive interruption rather than equating a visible stop button with complete revocability.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain effective, consequence-sensitive interruption rather than equating a visible stop button with complete revocability.; OPERATION: Provide a task-appropriate way to stop, revoke or restrict future action and to handle already-running actions according to their real interruption semantics.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A bounded one-shot harmless action may need no mid-action stop; existing actuator shutdown may already satisfy the need.
Problem
A person can nominally withdraw a task while queued or active actions continue beyond its valid scope.
Mechanism
Provide a task-appropriate way to stop, revoke or restrict future action and to handle already-running actions according to their real interruption semantics.
Trigger and context
Persistent delegation, changing user intent or an unsafe/unwanted execution trajectory.
Preconditions

EAA-042 — Objective uncertainty as a conditional route to deference (CONTESTED)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S040; DATE: 2017; WORK: The Off-Switch Game; VERSION: IJCAI 2017 pp.220–227; DOI 10.24963/ijcai.2017/32; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S041; DATE: 2025; WORK: The Partially Observable Off-Switch Game; VERSION: AAAI 2025 final proceedings PDF; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Utility uncertainty used to analyse off-switch incentives, later extended to private-information games.
Development
Retain the conditional incentive insight while rejecting its promotion to a universal corrigibility guarantee.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain the conditional incentive insight while rejecting its promotion to a universal corrigibility guarantee.; OPERATION: Represent uncertainty about the objective and the evidential value of human action, but determine deference under the actual information and interaction model rather than assuming uncertainty guarantees it.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Explicit externally enforced authority can be sufficient for a bounded task; no Bayesian game is required to honour an ordinary stop rule.
Problem
A utility-maximising agent may have no incentive to preserve human intervention when it treats its objective as settled.
Mechanism
Represent uncertainty about the objective and the evidential value of human action, but determine deference under the actual information and interaction model rather than assuming uncertainty guarantees it.
Trigger and context
Designing incentives for decisions shared between a human and an uncertain-utility assistant.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: A defensible utility/observation model and a justified account of human information and choices.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Agent incentive designer and reviewer

A defensible utility/observation model and a justified account of human information and choices.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Identify sender, recipient, consumer and non-response path; provide the task-relevant state needed for actual takeover. Consumer and prerequisite: Agent incentive designer and reviewer. A defensible utility/observation model and a justified account of human information and choices.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
Any claimed deference result is stated with the game and assumptions that entail it.

Explicit externally enforced authority can be sufficient for a bounded task; no Bayesian game is required to honour an ordinary stop rule.

EAA-043 — User autonomy, privacy and affected-party accountability (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S043; DATE: 1997; WORK: Software Agents and User Autonomy; VERSION: Proceedings of Autonomous Agents 1997, pp.466–469; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S021; DATE: undated author manuscript (workshop-era; exact year unverified); WORK: Electric Elves: What Went Wrong and Why; VERSION: Six-page author manuscript; distinct from the later 2008 AI Magazine article; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Normative distinctions concerning capability, knowledge, misrepresentation and fluid control, with deployment privacy lessons.
Development
Retain meaningful participation and accountability as design constraints, not a claim that all ethical conflicts have a technical solution.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain meaningful participation and accountability as design constraints, not a claim that all ethical conflicts have a technical solution.; OPERATION: Identify whose choices, information and resources are affected; make material action and delegation understandable and contestable, with privacy and scope constraints applied to communication and memory.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A minimal transparent fixed action can satisfy the need; avoid collecting or storing personal data with no task consumer.
Problem
An agent can advance a narrow task while reducing meaningful user control, exposing information or imposing unconsented costs on others.
Mechanism
Identify whose choices, information and resources are affected; make material action and delegation understandable and contestable, with privacy and scope constraints applied to communication and memory.
Trigger and context
Assistance or autonomous action affects people beyond internal computation.
Preconditions

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain recipient- and action-specific explanatory usefulness, not compulsory chain-of-thought narration or anthropomorphic persuasion.; OPERATION: Explain the action-relevant evidence, limits, uncertainty and available correction at the decision point; assess whether the recipient can use it, not merely whether a rationale is fluent.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Visible direct feedback or a simple status display may be more useful than a long explanation; no rationale is needed for an inconsequential self-evident action.
Problem
A plausible explanation or human-like persona can make people rely on behaviour they cannot actually predict or control.
Mechanism

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain claim-adequate evaluation and oracle criticism rather than mandatory model-on-model judging or a single reward.; OPERATION: Choose observations and tests adequate to the claimed outcome and protected process constraints; separate generated assertions from evaluation evidence, using independent checks where needed.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A trustworthy direct return contract or simple external predicate may suffice; a separate evaluator model is not inherently better.

The same mechanism that generates an answer can certify its own error, and a terminal-state score can omit prohibited actions.

EAA-046 — Repeated trials, versions and reproducible conditions (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S033; DATE: 2024-06; WORK: τ-bench: A Benchmark for Tool-Agent-User Interaction in Real-World Domains; VERSION: arXiv:2406.12045v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S035; DATE: 2024-07-01; WORK: AI Agents That Matter; VERSION: arXiv:2407.01502v1; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S032; DATE: 2024; WORK: WebArena: A Realistic Web Environment for Building Autonomous Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2307.13854v4; initial submission 2023; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Repeated evaluations with different success aggregations and disclosed model/task configurations.
Development
Retain reproducible measurement and dependence accounting, not one mandatory seed count or aggregate metric.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain reproducible measurement and dependence accounting, not one mandatory seed count or aggregate metric.; OPERATION: Report task units, attempts, resets, seeds/model/tool versions and aggregation; distinguish any-success sampling from success on every repeated trial, and preserve enough configuration for meaningful reproduction.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A deterministic bounded mechanism may need a smaller condition-coverage test rather than arbitrary repeated identical runs.
Problem
A single successful trajectory or best-of-many result can hide instability and cannot identify a dependable ongoing service.
Mechanism
Report task units, attempts, resets, seeds/model/tool versions and aggregation; distinguish any-success sampling from success on every repeated trial, and preserve enough configuration for meaningful reproduction.
Trigger and context
Stochastic agents, adaptive environments, comparative evaluation or reliability claims.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Defined trial units, controlled/reset state where appropriate and access to relevant configuration.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

EAA-049 — Protected tests and deliberate failure exposure (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin

EAA-050 — Monitor material assurance assumptions at runtime (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S042; DATE: 2018; WORK: Recognising Assumption Violations in Autonomous Systems Verification; VERSION: AAMAS 2018 extended abstract pp.1933–1935; SOURCE_ID: EAA-S022; DATE: 1999; WORK: Validating the DS1 Remote Agent Experiment; VERSION: ISAIRAS 1999 primary experiment report; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Runtime recognition of environmental assumption violations used in static verification.
Development
Retain useful assumption monitoring with explicit response and coverage, not an all-purpose wrapper that proves the entire world safe.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain useful assumption monitoring with explicit response and coverage, not an all-purpose wrapper that proves the entire world safe.; OPERATION: Monitor observable assumptions that matter to the guarantee and connect detected violations to an appropriate re-evaluation, fallback or limitation of the claim.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No runtime monitor is required for an immutable established condition, or where an existing boundary check already detects the relevant violation.
Problem
A verified decision component can operate outside the environmental conditions under which it was verified.
Mechanism
Monitor observable assumptions that matter to the guarantee and connect detected violations to an appropriate re-evaluation, fallback or limitation of the claim.
Trigger and context
Assurance depends on environmental behaviour that can change or fail.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer
Authority boundary
ACTOR_OR_CONSUMER: Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer; BOUNDARY: The relevant actor may act only through its real capability and current delegated scope. Changing means does not authorise changing protected goals, user constraints or another participant’s rights. Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.
Communication or interaction
FUNCTION: Disclose user simulation, human assistance and affected-party costs; protected tests must not externalise harm. Consumer and prerequisite: Runtime monitor, executor and assurance reviewer. Explicit assumptions, observable indicators and a consumer capable of acting on a violation.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.
Observable criterion
A violated monitored assumption invalidates the relevant assurance claim or changes operation rather than remaining a silent discrepancy.
Nontrigger or cheaper adequate path
No runtime monitor is required for an immutable established condition, or where an existing boundary check already detects the relevant violation.
Historical disposition
CONTEXT_DEPENDENT
Failure modes

Contrary evidence

Assumption sensitivity
Unobservable assumptions and unmodelled failures remain outside monitor coverage.
Unresolved questions

EAA-052 — Untrusted observations do not confer instruction authority (RETAINED_IN_EVOLVED_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S034; DATE: 2024-11-24; WORK: AgentDojo: A Dynamic Environment to Evaluate Prompt Injection Attacks and Defenses for LLM Agents; VERSION: arXiv:2406.13352v3; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Adversarial instructions embedded in externally returned tool content.
Development
Retain input-integrity and authority separation as a contemporary translation, not a claim that older belief annotations solve prompt injection.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain input-integrity and authority separation as a contemporary translation, not a claim that older belief annotations solve prompt injection.; OPERATION: Treat external content according to its evidential and authority role; constrain consequential tool use independently of persuasive wording and evaluate both utility and attack resistance.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: No language-level injection mechanism is needed for a closed typed sensor value with no instruction interpretation, though ordinary input validation may still matter.
Problem
Tool results or retrieved text can contain adversarial instructions that redirect the agent beyond the delegated task.
Mechanism
Treat external content according to its evidential and authority role; constrain consequential tool use independently of persuasive wording and evaluate both utility and attack resistance.
Trigger and context
Language-model agents consume web pages, documents, messages or tool results from untrusted sources.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.; PROPERTY_IDS: []; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.

Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.
Communication or interaction

Consumer and prerequisite: Tool boundary, action selector and security evaluator. Identified trust boundaries, controlled capabilities and tests for relevant adversarial observations.; LIMIT: No extra message, meeting or human approval ritual is required without a consequential recipient/decision. A sent request alone does not establish receipt, acceptance or action.

EAA-053 — Language-model reasoning–action feedback (CONTEXT_DEPENDENT)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S030; DATE: 2023; WORK: ReAct: Synergizing Reasoning and Acting in Language Models; VERSION: arXiv:2210.03629v3; initial submission 2022; ICLR 2023 work; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
ReAct interleaves generated reasoning and actual tool/environment observations.
Development
Retain feedback-coupled language action as one implementation family, not as a new origin of agency or evidence of BDI semantics.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain feedback-coupled language action as one implementation family, not as a new origin of agency or evidence of BDI semantics.; OPERATION: Interleave task-relevant generated reasoning or action selection with real tool/environment observations and use them to revise subsequent choices, with capability and authority boundaries outside mere narration.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A fixed workflow, direct tool invocation or reactive policy is sufficient when open-ended selection offers no demonstrated benefit.
Problem
A language-only chain can invent facts or continue a plan without observing what its tools actually did.
Mechanism
Interleave task-relevant generated reasoning or action selection with real tool/environment observations and use them to revise subsequent choices, with capability and authority boundaries outside mere narration.
Trigger and context
Language models choose multi-step tool actions under changing information.
Preconditions
CONDITIONS: Grounded tool interfaces, interpretable feedback, bounded action selection and an adequate completion oracle.; PROPERTY_IDS: EAA-021; EAA-023; EAA-026; EAA-045; EAA-052; RELATION_SEMANTICS: A REQUIRES edge is guarded; it does not require a dedicated module or owner for each referenced property.
Decision or consumer
Language-model action selector and executor

Original form

Development

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain conditional feedback-driven revision, not permanent introspection or guaranteed self-improvement.; OPERATION: Use a bounded review of observed outcomes to formulate a testable change for the next attempt; keep feedback validity separate from the generated lesson and assess actual improvement.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: A direct correction or fixed retry can suffice; omit reflection when there is no new evidence or no expected benefit.

EAA-061 — Mandatory continuous reflection (SUPERSEDED_BY_STRONGER_FORM)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S031; DATE: 2023-10-10; WORK: Reflexion: Language Agents with Verbal Reinforcement Learning; VERSION: arXiv:2303.11366v4; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
Analytical extrapolation from reflective methods to a mandatory always-on ritual.
Development
Superseded by EAA-054 external-feedback-anchored reflection and EAA-028 value-sensitive deliberation.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Superseded by EAA-054 external-feedback-anchored reflection and EAA-028 value-sensitive deliberation.; OPERATION: The superseded form reflects after every event or indefinitely, independent of feedback quality and marginal value.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a direct correction, fixed policy or bounded externally evaluated reflection only when it is expected to help.
Problem

Mechanism
The superseded form reflects after every event or indefinitely, independent of feedback quality and marginal value.
Trigger and context

Historical origin

Original form

Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain testable failure attribution and bounded revision, not a requirement to diagnose every failure completely before any safe response.; OPERATION: Use discriminating observations to separate action failure, observation error, model mismatch, bad evaluation, goal ambiguity and inadequate capability; change only an authorised relevant mechanism and test the revised claim.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Apply a known local correction when the cause is established; otherwise preserve uncertainty and avoid broad self-modification.

EAA-070 — Predecessor-criterion evaluation of self-modification (ASSUMPTION_SENSITIVE)
Historical origin
ORIGINAL_OR_INSPECTED_ANCHORS: SOURCE_ID: EAA-S053; DATE: 2016; WORK: Self-Modification of Policy and Utility Function in Rational Agents; VERSION: AGI 2016 conference version, 10 pages; DOI 10.1007/978-3-319-41649-6_1; ATTRIBUTION_LIMIT: These are the exact inspected anchors, not an unsupported assertion of unique priority or direct influence among all cited works.
Original form
A formal realistic value function evaluates anticipated policy changes under the predecessor utility rather than a successor’s rewritten utility.
Development
Retain a narrow formal guard against successor self-certification, not general warrant for autonomous self-revision.
Evolved form at historical freeze
FORM: Retain a narrow formal guard against successor self-certification, not general warrant for autonomous self-revision.; OPERATION: In the specified formal model, evaluate anticipated post-change policy consequences using the predecessor utility and modification-independent beliefs, rather than letting a successor utility certify itself.; SELECTION_OR_OMISSION: Use a fixed mechanism or externally reviewed bounded update when broad self-modification is unnecessary or its assumptions cannot be established.
Problem
Evaluating a change by the changed utility or ignoring its future policy effects can make self-modification appear beneficial for the wrong reason.
Mechanism
In the specified formal model, evaluate anticipated post-change policy consequences using the predecessor utility and modification-independent beliefs, rather than letting a successor utility certify itself.
Trigger and context
Explicit policy or utility self-modification under a model capable of representing its future effects.
